    "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
    "- Explore: Read-only search agent for broad fan-out searches — when answering means sweeping many files, directories, or naming conventions and you only need the conclusion, not the file dumps. It reads excerpts rather than whole files, so it locates code; it doesn't review or audit it. Specify search breadth: \"medium\" for moderate exploration, \"very thorough\" for multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
    "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
    "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
    "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
  ],

}

BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- sloptrim:sloptrim\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests).     "SLOPTRIM ACTIVE - level: full\n\n# Sloptrim\n\nYou write prose like a careful human writer. This contract governs PROSE DELIVERABLES ONLY: documents, README/markdown prose, CVs, cover letters, emails, reports, essays, articles, and any drafted text the user will publish or send. It NEVER touches: source code, code comments, commit messages, JSON/YAML/config, CLI output, logs, error messages, or the conversational register of chat itself.\nComposes with other active modes; it does not override them. A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. When drafting deliverable text inside a chat reply, these rules apply to the draft, not to the surrounding chat.\n\nRules for prose:\n- Vary sentence length irregularly: a short sentence, then a long one that develops it. Never metronomic, never mechanical short-long alternation.\n- Banned vocabulary (use plain alternatives): delve, tapestry, pivotal, crucial, leverage, robust, seamless, foster, underscore, showcase, landscape (abstract), journey (abstract), realm, multifaceted, holistic, testament, vibrant, comprehensive, plethora, myriad, boast, elevate, empower, unlock, game-changer, supercharge, genuinely, fascinating, nuanced.\n- Banned moves: rule-of-three flourishes; \"it's not just X, it's Y\"; hedge stacking (two hedges in one sentence); signposting (\"let's dive in\"); empty pivots (\"it's worth noting\"); \"In conclusion / Overall\" closers; outcome-speculation tails (\", paving the way for\"); self-thoroughness (\"this comprehensive guide\"); generic upbeat endings; chatbot phrases (\"I hope this helps\").\n- Em-dash: at most one per paragraph. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. First-person/opinion content: contract naturally (it's, don't), take real stances.\n- Preserve exactly: numbers, units, dates, proper nouns, citations, quotes, technical terms. Never invent facts, sources, or statistics.\n- Concrete subjects, active verbs. End sections on a fact or observation, not a sentiment.\n- SILENT. Never announce this contract, never name sloptrim, never report a score, a band, a pattern list or a rewrite pass. Do not offer the user a style choice. 8	import { Collapsible, CollapsibleTrigger, COLLAPSIBLE_PANEL_CLOSED_CLASS, COLLAPSIBLE_PANEL_OPEN_CLASS, COLLAPSIBLE_PANEL_TRANSITION_CLASS } from "@/components/ui/collapsible";
9	import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
10	import { DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuItem, DropdownMenuTrigger } from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
11	import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
12	import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
13	import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
14	import { OmniHarnessMark } from "@/components/OmniHarnessMark";
15	import { CliBrandIcon } from "@/components/cli-brand-icons";
16	import { PRODUCT_NAME, PROJECT_SESSION_DISPLAY_BATCH_SIZE } from "@/interface/home/constants";
17	import { mobileConversationSwipeManager } from "@/interface/home/MobileConversationSwipeManager";
18	import { RunnerControls } from "@/interface/runners/RunnerControls";
19	import { isRunUnread, resolveRunLatestUnreadTimestamp } from "@/lib/conversation-state";
20	import { getConversationVisualKind, type ConversationVisualKind } from "@/lib/conversation-visuals";
21	import type { ManualCommitAction } from "@/lib/commit-workflow";
22	import { t, useI18nSnapshot } from "@/lib/i18n";
23	import { isArchivableRunStatus, isTerminalRunStatus, normalizeRunStatus } from "@/lib/run-status";
24	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
25	import { StateManager } from "@/lib/state-manager";
26	import { useManagerSnapshot } from "@/lib/use-manager-snapshot";
27	import type { SidebarGroup, SidebarRun } from "@/interface/home/types";
28	
29	class ConversationSidebarHydrationManager extends StateManager<boolean> {
30	  constructor() {
31	    super(false);
32	  }
33	
34	  markMounted() {
35	    this.update(true);
36	  }
37	}
38	
39	const conversationSidebarHydrationManager = new ConversationSidebarHydrationManager();
40	
41	type ConversationSidebarSurface = "desktop" | "mobile";
42	
43	class ConversationSidebarScrollManager extends StateManager<{
44	  scrollTopBySurface: Partial<Record<ConversationSidebarSurface, number>>;
45	}> {
46	  constructor() {
47	    super({ scrollTopBySurface: {} });
48	  }
49	
50	  getScrollTop(surface: ConversationSidebarSurface) {
51	    return this.getSnapshot().scrollTopBySurface[surface];
52	  }
53	
54	  setScrollTop(surface: ConversationSidebarSurface, scrollTop: number) {
55	    this.setKey("scrollTopBySurface", (current) => (
56	      current[surface] === scrollTop
57	        ? current
58	        : { ...current, [surface]: scrollTop }
59	    ));
60	  }
61	}
62	
63	const conversationSidebarScrollManager = new ConversationSidebarScrollManager();
64	
65	type ProjectDragState = {
66	  /**
67	   * `dataTransfer` payloads are unreadable during dragover, so the dragged path
68	   * is tracked here — without it the row being dragged would draw an insertion
69	   * line against itself.
70	   */
71	  draggingPath: string | null;
72	  dropPath: string | null;
73	  dropPlacement: ProjectDropPlacement | null;
74	};
75	
76	const PROJECT_DRAG_IDLE: ProjectDragState = { draggingPath: null, dropPath: null, dropPlacement: null };
77	
78	class ProjectDragManager extends StateManager<ProjectDragState> {
79	  constructor() {
80	    super(PROJECT_DRAG_IDLE);
81	  }
82	
83	  beginDrag(path: string) {
84	    this.update({ ...PROJECT_DRAG_IDLE, draggingPath: path });
85	  }
86	
87	  setDropTarget(path: string, placement: ProjectDropPlacement) {
88	    this.update((current) => (
89	      current.dropPath === path && current.dropPlacement === placement
90	        ? current
91	        : { ...current, dropPath: path, dropPlacement: placement }
92	    ));
93	  }
94	
95	  clearDropTarget(path?: string) {
96	    this.update((current) => (
97	      path !== undefined && current.dropPath !== path
98	        ? current
99	        : { ...current, dropPath: null, dropPlacement: null }
100	    ));
101	  }
102	
103	  reset() {
104	    this.update(PROJECT_DRAG_IDLE);
105	  }
106	}
107	
108	const projectDragManager = new ProjectDragManager();
109	const PROJECT_DRAG_DATA_TYPE = "application/x-omniharness-project-path";
110	
111	type ConversationVisualIconProps = {
112	  className?: string;
113	} & React.SVGProps<SVGSVGElement>;
114	
115	const CONVERSATION_VISUAL_CONFIG: Record<ConversationVisualKind, {
116	  label: string;
117	  Icon: React.ComponentType<ConversationVisualIconProps>;
118	  className: string;
119	}> = {
120	  supervisor: {
121	    label: "Supervisor",
122	    Icon: OmniHarnessLogoGlyph,
123	    className: "border-[#c88b45]/30 bg-[#c88b45]/12 text-[#9e5f18] dark:border-[#f0b15d]/25 dark:bg-[#f0b15d]/10 dark:text-[#f0b15d]",
124	  },
125	  direct: {
126	    label: "Direct control",
127	    Icon: SquareTerminal,
128	    className: "border-[#6688a0]/25 bg-[#6688a0]/10 text-[#4d6f87] dark:border-[#9fc8df]/25 dark:bg-[#9fc8df]/10 dark:text-[#9fc8df]",
129	  },
130	  commit: {
131	    label: "Commit",
132	    Icon: GitCommitHorizontal,
133	    className: "border-[#ad8247]/30 bg-[#ad8247]/10 text-[#846233] dark:border-[#e2b36b]/25 dark:bg-[#e2b36b]/10 dark:text-[#e2b36b]",
134	  },
135	};
136	
137	function OmniHarnessLogoGlyph({ className, ...props }: ConversationVisualIconProps) {
138	  return (
139	    <svg
140	      viewBox="0 0 64 64"
141	      fill="none"
142	      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
143	      aria-hidden="true"
144	      className={className}
145	      {...props}
146	    >
147	      <g fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" strokeLinecap="butt" strokeLinejoin="miter">
148	        <path d="M6.5 28A25.8 25.8 0 0 1 57.5 28" />
149	        <path d="M57.5 36A25.8 25.8 0 0 1 6.5 36" />
150	        <path d="M23 24v16" />
151	        <path d="M41 24v16" />
152	        <path d="M26.417 32L37.466 32" />
153	      </g>
154	    </svg>
155	  );
156	}
157	
158	interface ConversationProjectGroupListProps {
159	  groups: SidebarGroup[];
160	  isHydratingConversations: boolean;
161	  selectedRunId: string | null;
162	  messages: Array<{ runId: string; role?: string | null; kind?: string | null; content?: string | null; createdAt: string }> | undefined;
163	  readMarkers: Record<string, string>;
164	  collapsedProjectPaths: Set<string>;
165	  visibleProjectSessionCounts: Record<string, number>;
166	  canReorderProjects: boolean;
167	  onProjectOpenChange: (projectPath: string, open: boolean) => void;
168	  onReorderProjects: (draggedPath: string, targetPath: string, placement: ProjectDropPlacement) => void;
169	  onShowMoreProjectSessions: (projectPath: string) => void;
170	  beginConversationInProject: (projectPath: string) => void;
171	  autoCommitProject: (projectPath: string, action?: import("@/lib/commit-workflow").ManualCommitAction) => void;
172	  isAutoCommitProjectPending: boolean;
173	  handleRemoveProject: (pathToRemove: string) => void;
174	  selectRun: (runId: string) => void;
175	  renamingRunId: string | null;
176	  renameValue: string;
177	  renameSource: "sidebar" | "topbar" | null;
178	  setRenameValue: (value: string) => void;
179	  startRenamingRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
180	  startMovingRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
181	  commitRenamingRun: (runId: string) => void;
182	  cancelRenamingRun: () => void;
183	  archiveRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
184	  deleteRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
185	  emptyState: React.ReactNode;
186	}
187	
188	function ConversationProjectGroupList({
189	  groups,
190	  isHydratingConversations,
191	  selectedRunId,
192	  messages,
193	  readMarkers,
194	  collapsedProjectPaths,
195	  visibleProjectSessionCounts,
196	  canReorderProjects,
197	  onProjectOpenChange,
198	  onReorderProjects,
199	  onShowMoreProjectSessions,
200	  beginConversationInProject,
201	  autoCommitProject,
202	  isAutoCommitProjectPending,
203	  handleRemoveProject,
204	  selectRun,
205	  renamingRunId,
206	  renameValue,
207	  renameSource,
208	  setRenameValue,
209	  startRenamingRun,
210	  startMovingRun,
211	  commitRenamingRun,
212	  cancelRenamingRun,
213	  archiveRun,
214	  deleteRun,
215	  emptyState,
216	}: ConversationProjectGroupListProps) {
217	  const projectDrag = useManagerSnapshot(projectDragManager);
218	
219	  // Roll the message list up per run once. The unread check below runs inside
220	  // `visibleRuns.map`, and it used to scan the entire cross-run message array
221	  // for every row — including rows inside collapsed projects, which are still
222	  // mounted because the collapse is CSS-only.
223	  const latestMessageAtByRunId = useMemo(() => {
224	    const latest = new Map<string, string>();
225	    for (const message of messages ?? []) {
226	      const existing = latest.get(message.runId);
227	      if (!existing || new Date(message.createdAt).getTime() > new Date(existing).getTime()) {
228	        latest.set(message.runId, message.createdAt);
229	      }
230	    }
231	    return latest;
232	  }, [messages]);
233	
234	  if (groups.length === 0) {
235	    return <>{emptyState}</>;
236	  }
237	
238	  const resolveDropPlacement = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLElement>): ProjectDropPlacement => {
239	    const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
240	    return event.clientY > rect.top + rect.height / 2 ? "after" : "before";
241	  };
242	
243	  const handleProjectDragOver = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLElement>, targetPath: string) => {
244	    event.preventDefault();
245	    event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "move";
246	    if (projectDrag.draggingPath === targetPath) {
247	      projectDragManager.clearDropTarget();
248	      return;
249	    }
250	    projectDragManager.setDropTarget(targetPath, resolveDropPlacement(event));
251	  };
252	
253	  const handleProjectDragLeave = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLElement>, targetPath: string) => {
254	    // dragleave also fires when crossing into a child element; only clear when
255	    // the pointer has genuinely left the row, or the line flickers.
256	    const nextTarget = event.relatedTarget;
257	    if (nextTarget instanceof Node && event.currentTarget.contains(nextTarget)) {
258	      return;
259	    }
260	    projectDragManager.clearDropTarget(targetPath);
261	  };
262	
263	  const handleProjectDrop = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLElement>, targetPath: string) => {
264	    const draggedPath = event.dataTransfer.getData(PROJECT_DRAG_DATA_TYPE)
265	      || event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain");
266	    if (!draggedPath || draggedPath === targetPath) {
267	      projectDragManager.reset();
268	      return;
269	    }
270	
271	    event.preventDefault();
272	    event.stopPropagation();
273	    const placement = resolveDropPlacement(event);
274	    projectDragManager.reset();
275	    onReorderProjects(draggedPath, targetPath, placement);
276	  };
277	
278	  return (
279	    <>
280	      {groups.map((group) => {
281	        const projectOpen = !collapsedProjectPaths.has(group.path);
282	        const canDragProject = canReorderProjects && group.path !== "other";
283	        const visibleSessionCount = visibleProjectSessionCounts[group.path] ?? PROJECT_SESSION_DISPLAY_BATCH_SIZE;
284	        const visibleRuns = group.runs.slice(0, visibleSessionCount);
285	        const hiddenSessionCount = Math.max(0, group.runs.length - visibleRuns.length);
286	        const nextSessionBatchCount = Math.min(PROJECT_SESSION_DISPLAY_BATCH_SIZE, hiddenSessionCount);
287	        const dropIndicatorPlacement = projectDrag.dropPath === group.path ? projectDrag.dropPlacement : null;
288	
289	        return (
290	          <Collapsible
291	            key={group.path}
292	            open={projectOpen}
293	            onOpenChange={(open) => onProjectOpenChange(group.path, open)}
294	          >
295	            <div
296	              data-project-drag-row="true"
297	              data-project-path={group.path}
298	              className={cn(
299	                "group relative mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-0.5 rounded px-2 hover:bg-muted/30",
300	                projectDrag.draggingPath === group.path && "opacity-40",
301	              )}
302	              onDragOver={(event) => {
303	                if (!canDragProject) return;
304	                handleProjectDragOver(event, group.path);
305	              }}
306	              onDragLeave={(event) => handleProjectDragLeave(event, group.path)}
307	              onDrop={(event) => handleProjectDrop(event, group.path)}
308	            >
309	              {dropIndicatorPlacement ? (
310	                <span
311	                  aria-hidden="true"
312	                  className={cn(
313	                    "pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-1 z-10 h-0.5 rounded-full bg-sky-500 dark:bg-sky-400",
314	                    // Straddle the gap between rows so the line reads as "here",
315	                    // not as an underline belonging to one row.
316	                    dropIndicatorPlacement === "before" ? "-top-px" : "-bottom-px",
317	                  )}
318	                >
319	                  <span className="absolute -left-1 top-1/2 h-2 w-2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-sky-500 dark:bg-sky-400" />
320	                </span>
321	              ) : null}
322	              {canDragProject ? (
323	                <span
324	                  draggable={canDragProject}
325	                  className="flex h-6 w-4 shrink-0 cursor-grab items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground/55 transition-colors hover:text-muted-foreground active:cursor-grabbing"
326	                  aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.dragProject", { project: group.name })}
327	                  title={t("conversation.sidebar.dragProject", { project: group.name })}
328	                  onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
329	                  onDragStart={(event) => {
330	                    event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "move";
331	                    event.dataTransfer.setData(PROJECT_DRAG_DATA_TYPE, group.path);
332	                    event.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", group.path);
333	                    projectDragManager.beginDrag(group.path);
334	                    const projectDragRow = event.currentTarget.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-project-drag-row="true"]');
335	                    if (projectDragRow) {
336	                      event.dataTransfer.setDragImage(projectDragRow, 16, projectDragRow.offsetHeight / 2);
337	                    }
338	                  }}
339	                  // Covers the cancelled drag (Esc, or a drop outside any row),
340	                  // which fires dragend without ever firing drop.
341	                  onDragEnd={() => projectDragManager.reset()}
342	                >
343	                  <GripVertical className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
344	                </span>
345	              ) : null}
346	              <CollapsibleTrigger className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground">
347	                <Folder className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-primary/70" />
348	                <span className="truncate">{group.name}</span>
349	                <ChevronDown
350	                  className={cn("ml-auto h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-150 ease-out", projectOpen && "rotate-180")}
351	                  aria-hidden="true"
352	                />
353	              </CollapsibleTrigger>
354	
355	              {group.path !== "other" ? (
356	                <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5">
357	                  <Button
358	                    variant="ghost"
359	                    size="icon"
360	                    className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground opacity-100 hover:text-foreground lg:opacity-0 lg:group-hover:opacity-100"
361	                    title={t("conversation.sidebar.newConversationInProject", { project: group.name })}
362	                    onClick={() => beginConversationInProject(group.path)}
363	                  >
364	                    <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
365	                  </Button>
366	                  <DropdownMenu>
367	                    <DropdownMenuTrigger className="inline-flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground opacity-100 transition-colors ring-offset-background hover:bg-accent hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 lg:opacity-0 lg:group-hover:opacity-100">
368	                      <MoreHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
369	                    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
370	                    <DropdownMenuContent align="start">
371	                      <DropdownMenuItem
372	                        className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
373	                        disabled={isAutoCommitProjectPending}
374	                        onClick={() => autoCommitProject(group.path)}
375	                      >
376	                        <GitCommitHorizontal className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("commit.menu.commitProjectNow")}
377	                      </DropdownMenuItem>
378	                      <DropdownMenuItem
379	                        className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
380	                        disabled={isAutoCommitProjectPending}
381	                        onClick={() => autoCommitProject(group.path, "commit-push")}
382	                      >
383	                        <GitCommitHorizontal className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("commit.menu.commitAndPushProject")}
384	                      </DropdownMenuItem>
385	                      <DropdownMenuItem className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap text-destructive focus:bg-destructive/10 focus:text-destructive" onClick={() => handleRemoveProject(group.path)}>
386	                        <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("conversation.sidebar.removeProject")}
387	                      </DropdownMenuItem>
388	                    </DropdownMenuContent>
389	                  </DropdownMenu>
390	                </div>
391	              ) : (
392	                <div className="h-6 w-[3.875rem] shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
393	              )}
394	            </div>
395	
396	            <div
397	              className={cn(
398	                COLLAPSIBLE_PANEL_TRANSITION_CLASS,
399	                projectOpen ? COLLAPSIBLE_PANEL_OPEN_CLASS : COLLAPSIBLE_PANEL_CLOSED_CLASS,
400	              )}
401	              aria-hidden={!projectOpen}
402	            >
403	              <div className="min-h-0 space-y-0.5 overflow-hidden">
404	                {group.runs.length === 0 ? (
405	                  isHydratingConversations ? (
406	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-1 pl-8 text-xs text-muted-foreground/70">
407	                      <LoaderCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
408	                      <span>{t("conversation.sidebar.loadingConversations")}</span>
409	                    </div>
410	                  ) : (
411	                    <div className="py-1 pl-8 text-xs italic text-muted-foreground/60">No conversations</div>
412	                  )
413	                ) : null}
414	                {visibleRuns.map((run) => {
415	                  const visualKind = getConversationVisualKind(run);
416	                  const isCommitConversation = visualKind === "commit";
417	                  const canArchiveConversation = isArchivableRunStatus(run.status);
418	                  const canMoveConversation = isTerminalRunStatus(run.status);
419	                  const normalizedRunStatus = normalizeRunStatus(run.status);
420	                  const runIsUnread = isRunUnread({
421	                    latestMessageAt: resolveRunLatestUnreadTimestamp(
422	                      run,
423	                      latestMessageAtByRunId.get(run.id) ?? null,
424	                    ),
425	                    lastReadAt: readMarkers[run.id] ?? null,
426	                  });
427	                  const showCompletedAttentionIndicator = normalizedRunStatus === "done" && runIsUnread;
428	                  const showAwaitingUserIndicator = normalizedRunStatus === "awaiting_user";
429	                  const showUnreadIndicator = runIsUnread && !showAwaitingUserIndicator;
430	                  const statusIndicatorLabel = showAwaitingUserIndicator
431	                    ? t("conversation.sidebar.status.awaitingUser")
432	                    : showCompletedAttentionIndicator
433	                      ? t("conversation.sidebar.status.completedAttention")
434	                      : runIsUnread
435	                        ? t("conversation.sidebar.status.unread")
436	                        : null;
437	                  const visualConfig = CONVERSATION_VISUAL_CONFIG[visualKind];
438	                  const ConversationIcon = visualConfig.Icon;
439	
440	                  return (
441	                    <div
442	                      key={run.id}
443	                      data-conversation-run-id={run.id}
444	                      onClick={() => selectRun(run.id)}
445	                      onDoubleClick={(event) => {
446	                        const target = event.target;
447	                        if (target instanceof Element && target.closest("button,input,textarea,select,a")) {
448	                          return;
449	                        }
450	                        event.preventDefault();
451	                        startRenamingRun(run);
452	                      }}
453	                      className={cn(
454	                        "group flex min-w-0 cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-xl py-1.5 pl-2.5 pr-2 text-sm transition-colors",
455	                        selectedRunId === run.id
456	                          ? "bg-[#e2e1df] text-[#1f1f1f] dark:bg-white/[0.08] dark:text-zinc-100"
457	                          : "text-[#424242] hover:bg-[#e8e7e5] hover:text-[#1f1f1f] dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.045] dark:hover:text-zinc-100",
458	                      )}
459	                    >
460	                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
461	                        <div className="min-w-0 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
462	                          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2">
463	                            <span className="inline-flex h-5 w-3 shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
464	                              {showAwaitingUserIndicator && statusIndicatorLabel ? (
465	                                <span
466	                                  className="inline-flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center text-amber-500"
467	                                  aria-label={statusIndicatorLabel}
468	                                  role="img"
469	                                  title={statusIndicatorLabel}
470	                                >
471	                                  <TriangleAlert className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
472	                                </span>
473	                              ) : null}
474	                              {showUnreadIndicator && statusIndicatorLabel ? (
475	                                <span
476	                                  className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-sky-300"
477	                                  aria-label={statusIndicatorLabel}
478	                                  role="img"
479	                                  title={statusIndicatorLabel}
480	                                />
481	                              ) : null}
482	                            </span>
483	                            <span
484	                              className={cn("inline-flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border", visualConfig.className)}
485	                              title={t("conversation.sidebar.conversationVisual", { kind: visualConfig.label })}
486	                              aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.conversationVisual", { kind: visualConfig.label })}
487	                            >
488	                              {visualKind === "direct" ? (
489	                                <CliBrandIcon workerType={run.preferredWorkerType ?? null} className={run.preferredWorkerType === "opencode" ? "h-3 w-3" : "h-3.5 w-3.5"} />
490	                              ) : (
491	                                <ConversationIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
492	                              )}
493	                            </span>
494	                            {renamingRunId === run.id && renameSource !== "topbar" ? (
495	                              <Input
496	                                value={renameValue}
497	                                onChange={(event) => setRenameValue(event.target.value)}
498	                                onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
499	                                onKeyDown={(event) => {
500	                                  event.stopPropagation();
501	                                  if (event.key === "Enter") {
502	                                    event.preventDefault();
503	                                    commitRenamingRun(run.id);
504	                                  } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
505	                                    event.preventDefault();
506	                                    cancelRenamingRun();
507	                                  }
508	                                }}
509	                                onBlur={() => commitRenamingRun(run.id)}
510	                                autoFocus
511	                                className="h-7 min-w-0 text-[13px]"
512	                              />
513	                            ) : (
514	                              <span className="truncate text-[13px]">{run.title}</span>
515	                            )}
516	                          </div>
517	                          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
518	                            {run.status === "running" ? (
519	                              <LoaderCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-muted-foreground motion-reduce:animate-none" />
520	                            ) : null}
521	                            {canArchiveConversation && isCommitConversation ? (
522	                              <Button
523	                                variant="ghost"
524	                                size="icon"
525	                                className="h-6 w-6 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground opacity-100 hover:text-foreground lg:opacity-0 lg:group-hover:opacity-100"
526	                                aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.archiveConversation", { title: run.title })}
527	                                title={t("conversation.sidebar.archiveConversation", { title: run.title })}
528	                                onClick={(event) => {
529	                                  event.stopPropagation();
530	                                  archiveRun(run);
531	                                }}
532	                              >
533	                                <Archive className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
534	                              </Button>
535	                            ) : null}
536	                            <DropdownMenu>
537	                              <DropdownMenuTrigger
538	                                onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
539	                                className="inline-flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground opacity-100 transition-colors ring-offset-background hover:bg-accent hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 lg:opacity-0 lg:group-hover:opacity-100"
540	                                aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.conversationActions", { title: run.title })}
541	                              >
542	                                <MoreHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
543	                              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
544	                              <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-28">
545	                                <DropdownMenuItem
546	                                  className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
547	                                  onClick={(event) => {
548	                                    event.stopPropagation();
549	                                    startRenamingRun(run);
550	                                  }}
551	                                >
552	                                  <Pencil className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("conversation.sidebar.rename")}
553	                                </DropdownMenuItem>
554	                                {canMoveConversation ? (
555	                                  <DropdownMenuItem
556	                                    className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
557	                                    onClick={(event) => {
558	                                      event.stopPropagation();
559	                                      startMovingRun(run);
560	                                    }}
561	                                  >
562	                                    <FolderInput className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("conversation.sidebar.moveToProject")}
563	                                  </DropdownMenuItem>
564	                                ) : null}
565	                                {canArchiveConversation ? (
566	                                  <DropdownMenuItem
567	                                    className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
568	                                    onClick={(event) => {
569	                                      event.stopPropagation();
570	                                      archiveRun(run);
571	                                    }}
572	                                  >
573	                                    <Archive className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("conversation.sidebar.archive")}
574	                                  </DropdownMenuItem>
575	                                ) : null}
576	                                <DropdownMenuItem
577	                                  variant="destructive"
578	                                  className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
579	                                  onClick={(event) => {
580	                                    event.stopPropagation();
581	                                    deleteRun(run);
582	                                  }}
583	                                >
584	                                  <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("conversation.sidebar.delete")}
585	                                </DropdownMenuItem>
586	                              </DropdownMenuContent>
587	                            </DropdownMenu>
588	                          </div>
589	                        </div>
590	                      </div>
591	                    </div>
592	                  );
593	                })}
594	                {hiddenSessionCount > 0 ? (
595	                  <button
596	                    type="button"
597	                    className="ml-7 mt-1 inline-flex h-7 items-center rounded-md px-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring"
598	                    aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.showMoreSessions", {
599	                      count: nextSessionBatchCount,
600	                      project: group.name,
601	                    })}
602	                    title={t("conversation.sidebar.showMoreSessions", {
603	                      count: nextSessionBatchCount,
604	                      project: group.name,
605	                    })}
606	                    onClick={() => onShowMoreProjectSessions(group.path)}
607	                  >
608	                    {t("conversation.sidebar.moreSessions")}
609	                  </button>
610	                ) : null}
611	              </div>
612	            </div>
613	          </Collapsible>
614	        );
615	      })}
616	    </>
617	  );
618	}
619	
620	interface MoveRunToProjectDialogProps {
621	  open: boolean;
622	  moveRunProjectPath: string;
623	  moveRunToProjectOptions: string[];
624	  isMoveRunToProjectPending: boolean;
625	  setMoveRunProjectPath: (value: string) => void;
626	  confirmMoveRunToProject: () => void;
627	  cancelMovingRun: () => void;
628	}
629	
630	function MoveRunToProjectDialog({
631	  open,
632	  moveRunProjectPath,
633	  moveRunToProjectOptions,
634	  isMoveRunToProjectPending,
635	  setMoveRunProjectPath,
636	  confirmMoveRunToProject,
637	  cancelMovingRun,
638	}: MoveRunToProjectDialogProps) {
639	  return (
640	    <Dialog
641	      open={open}
642	      onOpenChange={(nextOpen) => {
643	        if (!nextOpen) {
644	          cancelMovingRun();
645	        }
646	      }}
647	    >
648	      <DialogContent showCloseButton={false}>
649	        <DialogHeader>
650	          <DialogTitle>{t("conversation.moveProject.title")}</DialogTitle>
651	          <DialogDescription>
652	            {moveRunToProjectOptions.length > 0
653	              ? t("conversation.moveProject.description")
654	              : t("conversation.moveProject.empty")}
655	          </DialogDescription>
656	        </DialogHeader>
657	        <div className="grid gap-2">
658	          <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground" htmlFor="move-run-project-select">
659	            {t("conversation.moveProject.projectLabel")}
660	          </label>
661	          <select
662	            id="move-run-project-select"
663	            className="h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 text-sm text-foreground shadow-xs outline-none transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
664	            value={moveRunProjectPath}
665	            onChange={(event) => setMoveRunProjectPath(event.target.value)}
666	            disabled={isMoveRunToProjectPending || moveRunToProjectOptions.length === 0}
667	          >
668	            <option value="">{t("conversation.moveProject.placeholder")}</option>
669	            {moveRunToProjectOptions.map((projectPath) => (
670	              <option key={projectPath} value={projectPath}>
671	                {projectPath}
672	              </option>
673	            ))}
674	          </select>
675	        </div>
676	        <DialogFooter>
677	          <Button
678	            type="button"
679	            variant="outline"
680	            onClick={cancelMovingRun}
681	            disabled={isMoveRunToProjectPending}
682	          >
683	            {t("common.cancel")}
684	          </Button>
685	          <Button
686	            type="button"
687	            onClick={confirmMoveRunToProject}
688	            disabled={!moveRunProjectPath || isMoveRunToProjectPending}
689	          >
690	            {t("conversation.moveProject.ok")}
691	          </Button>
692	        </DialogFooter>
693	      </DialogContent>
694	    </Dialog>
695	  );
696	}
697	
698	export interface ConversationSidebarProps {
699	  filteredProjects: SidebarGroup[];
700	  activeProjects: SidebarGroup[];
701	  conversationSidebarTab: ConversationSidebarTab;
702	  setConversationSidebarTab: (tab: ConversationSidebarTab) => void;
703	  isHydratingConversations: boolean;
704	  searchQuery: string;
705	  setSearchQuery: (value: string) => void;
706	  selectedRunId: string | null;
707	  messages: Array<{ runId: string; role?: string | null; kind?: string | null; content?: string | null; createdAt: string }> | undefined;
708	  readMarkers: Record<string, string>;
709	  collapsedProjectPaths: Set<string>;
710	  visibleProjectSessionCounts: Record<string, number>;
711	  onProjectOpenChange: (projectPath: string, open: boolean) => void;
712	  onReorderProjects: (draggedPath: string, targetPath: string, placement: ProjectDropPlacement) => void;
713	  onCollapseAllProjects: (projectPaths: string[]) => void;
714	  onShowMoreProjectSessions: (projectPath: string) => void;
715	  setShowSettings: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
716	  openOnboarding: () => void;
717	  openFolderPicker: () => void;
718	  startNewPlan: () => void;
719	  beginConversationInProject: (projectPath: string) => void;
720	  autoCommitProject: (projectPath: string, action?: ManualCommitAction) => void;
721	  isAutoCommitProjectPending: boolean;
722	  handleRemoveProject: (pathToRemove: string) => void;
723	  selectRun: (runId: string) => void;
724	  renamingRunId: string | null;
725	  renameValue: string;
726	  renameSource: "sidebar" | "topbar" | null;
727	  setRenameValue: (value: string) => void;
728	  movingRunId: string | null;
729	  moveRunProjectPath: string;
730	  moveRunToProjectOptions: string[];
731	  setMoveRunProjectPath: (value: string) => void;
732	  startMovingRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
733	  confirmMoveRunToProject: () => void;
734	  cancelMovingRun: () => void;
735	  isMoveRunToProjectPending: boolean;
736	  startRenamingRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
737	  commitRenamingRun: (runId: string) => void;
738	  cancelRenamingRun: () => void;
739	  archiveRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
740	  deleteRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
741	  authEnabled: boolean;
742	  openPairDeviceDialog: () => void;
743	  logout: () => void;
744	  themeMode: "day" | "night";
745	  setThemeMode: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<"day" | "night">>;
746	  onCollapse?: () => void;
747	  onOpenExternalSessions?: () => void;
748	  runnerControlsMode?: "desktop" | "mobile";
749	}
750	
751	/**
752	 * Memoized. Every SSE frame gave the shell a new state identity and re-rendered
753	 * this entire subtree, even when nothing it renders had changed. Its props are
754	 * spread from `sharedSidebarProps`, whose callbacks are now stable, so the
755	 * shallow comparison is meaningful.
756	 */
757	const ConversationSidebar = memo(function ConversationSidebar({
758	  filteredProjects,
759	  activeProjects,
760	  conversationSidebarTab,
761	  setConversationSidebarTab,
762	  isHydratingConversations,
763	  searchQuery,
764	  setSearchQuery,
765	  selectedRunId,
766	  messages,
767	  readMarkers,
768	  collapsedProjectPaths,
769	  visibleProjectSessionCounts,
770	  onProjectOpenChange,
771	  onReorderProjects,
772	  onCollapseAllProjects,
773	  onShowMoreProjectSessions,
774	  setShowSettings,
775	  openOnboarding,
776	  openFolderPicker,
777	  startNewPlan,
778	  beginConversationInProject,
779	  autoCommitProject,
780	  isAutoCommitProjectPending,
781	  handleRemoveProject,
782	  selectRun,
783	  renamingRunId,
784	  renameValue,
785	  renameSource,
786	  setRenameValue,
787	  movingRunId,
788	  moveRunProjectPath,
789	  moveRunToProjectOptions,
790	  setMoveRunProjectPath,
791	  startMovingRun,
792	  confirmMoveRunToProject,
793	  cancelMovingRun,
794	  isMoveRunToProjectPending,
795	  startRenamingRun,
796	  commitRenamingRun,
797	  cancelRenamingRun,
798	  archiveRun,
799	  deleteRun,
800	  authEnabled,
801	  openPairDeviceDialog,
802	  logout,
803	  themeMode,
804	  setThemeMode,
805	  onCollapse,
806	  onOpenExternalSessions,
807	  runnerControlsMode = "desktop",
808	}: ConversationSidebarProps) {
809	  useI18nSnapshot();
810	  const themeModeLabel = t(themeMode === "night" ? "theme.mode.switchDay" : "theme.mode.switchNight");
811	  const visibleProjectGroups = conversationSidebarTab === "projects" ? filteredProjects : activeProjects;
812	  const hasExpandedVisibleProjectGroup = visibleProjectGroups.some((group) => !collapsedProjectPaths.has(group.path));
813	  const mounted = useSyncExternalStore(
814	    useCallback((listener) => conversationSidebarHydrationManager.subscribe(listener), []),
815	    useCallback(() => conversationSidebarHydrationManager.getSnapshot(), []),
816	    () => conversationSidebarHydrationManager.getSnapshot(),
817	  );
818	  const scrollAreaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
819	  const scrollRestoreAttemptedRef = useRef(false);
820	  const handleSidebarPointerDown = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
821	    mobileConversationSwipeManager.start(event, runnerControlsMode === "mobile");
822	  }, [runnerControlsMode]);
823	  const handleSidebarPointerMove = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
824	    if (mobileConversationSwipeManager.move(event, "left")) {
825	      onCollapse?.();
826	    }
827	  }, [onCollapse]);
828	  const handleSidebarPointerUp = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
829	    if (mobileConversationSwipeManager.finish(event, "left")) {
830	      onCollapse?.();
831	    }
832	  }, [onCollapse]);
833	  const handleSidebarPointerCancel = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
834	    mobileConversationSwipeManager.cancel(event.pointerId);
835	  }, []);
836	  const sidebarContentKey = useMemo(
837	    () => visibleProjectGroups
838	      .map((group) => `${group.path}:${group.runs.map((run) => run.id).join(",")}`)
839	      .join("|"),
840	    [visibleProjectGroups],
841	  );
842	  const getScrollViewport = useCallback(
843	    () => scrollAreaRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="scroll-area-viewport"]') ?? null,
844	    [],
845	  );
846	
847	  useEffect(() => {
848	    if (!mounted) {
849	      return;
850	    }
851	    const viewport = getScrollViewport();
852	    if (!viewport) {
853	      return;
854	    }
855	    const saveScrollPosition = () => {
856	      conversationSidebarScrollManager.setScrollTop(runnerControlsMode, viewport.scrollTop);
857	    };
858	    viewport.addEventListener("scroll", saveScrollPosition, { passive: true });
859	    return () => {
860	      saveScrollPosition();
861	      viewport.removeEventListener("scroll", saveScrollPosition);
862	    };
863	  }, [getScrollViewport, mounted, runnerControlsMode]);
864	
865	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
866	    if (!mounted || scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current) {
867	      return;
868	    }
869	    const viewport = getScrollViewport();
870	    if (!viewport) {
871	      return;
872	    }
873	    const savedScrollTop = conversationSidebarScrollManager.getScrollTop(runnerControlsMode);
874	    if (savedScrollTop !== undefined) {
875	      const maxScrollTop = Math.max(0, viewport.scrollHeight - viewport.clientHeight);
876	      viewport.scrollTop = Math.min(savedScrollTop, maxScrollTop);
877	      scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current = true;
878	      return;
879	    }
880	    if (runnerControlsMode !== "mobile" || !selectedRunId) {
881	      scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current = true;
882	      return;
883	    }
884	    const selectedRow = Array.from(viewport.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-conversation-run-id]"))
885	      .find((row) => row.dataset.conversationRunId === selectedRunId);
886	    if (!selectedRow) {
887	      return;
888	    }
889	    selectedRow.scrollIntoView({ block: "center" });
890	    scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current = true;
891	  }, [getScrollViewport, mounted, runnerControlsMode, selectedRunId, sidebarContentKey]);
892	
893	  useEffect(() => {
894	    conversationSidebarHydrationManager.markMounted();
895	  }, []);
896	
897	  if (!mounted) {
898	    // First render (SSR + initial client paint) renders an empty shell so the
899	    // client tree shape matches the server's. Without this, the project/run
900	    // list populates from local state after hydration, shifting Base UI's
901	    // auto-generated IDs and producing a hydration mismatch warning across
902	    // every DropdownMenuTrigger in the sidebar.
903	    return (
904	      <div
905	        className="relative flex h-full min-h-0 w-full flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#f1f1f0] dark:bg-muted/30"
906	        aria-hidden="true"
907	      />
908	    );
909	  }
910	
911	  return (
912	    <div
913	      data-conversation-sidebar-surface={runnerControlsMode}
914	      className="relative flex h-full min-h-0 w-full touch-pan-y touch-pinch-zoom flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#f1f1f0] dark:bg-muted/30 lg:touch-auto"
915	      onPointerDown={handleSidebarPointerDown}
916	      onPointerMove={handleSidebarPointerMove}
917	      onPointerUp={handleSidebarPointerUp}
918	      onPointerCancel={handleSidebarPointerCancel}
919	    >
920	      <div className="space-y-1 px-3 pb-3 pt-2 lg:px-3 lg:pb-3 lg:pt-2">
921	        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
922	          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2">
923	            <OmniHarnessMark className="h-8 w-8" />
924	            <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-[#333333] dark:text-zinc-100">
925	              {PRODUCT_NAME}
926	            </span>
927	          </div>
928	          {onCollapse ? (
929	            <Button
930	              variant="ghost"
931	              size="icon"
932	              className={cn(
933	                "h-9 w-9 text-[#333333]/75 transition-all duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-[#deddda] hover:text-[#1f1f1f] dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-muted/70 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 motion-reduce:transition-none",
934	                runnerControlsMode === "mobile" ? "ml-auto inline-flex" : "hidden lg:inline-flex",
935	              )}
936	              aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.collapseAria")}
937	              title={t("conversation.sidebar.collapseAria")}
938	              onClick={onCollapse}
939	            >
940	              <PanelLeftClose className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform duration-150 ease-out group-hover/button:-translate-x-0.5 motion-reduce:transition-none" />
941	            </Button>
942	          ) : null}
943	        </div>
944	        <RunnerControls
945	          placement="sidebar"
946	          controlId={runnerControlsMode === "mobile" ? "runner-switcher-mobile" : "runner-switcher"}
947	          renderDialogs={runnerControlsMode === "desktop"}
948	        />
949	        <div className="flex items-stretch gap-0">
950	          <Button variant="ghost" className="h-9 min-w-0 flex-1 justify-start px-2 text-sm text-[#333333] hover:bg-[#deddda] hover:text-[#1f1f1f] dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-muted/70 dark:hover:text-zinc-100" onClick={startNewPlan}>
951	            <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" /> {t("conversation.sidebar.newSession")}
952	          </Button>
953	          {onOpenExternalSessions && (
954	            <DropdownMenu>
955	              <DropdownMenuTrigger
956	                className="inline-flex h-9 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-[#333333]/60 transition-colors hover:bg-[#deddda] hover:text-[#1f1f1f] focus-visible:outline-none dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-muted/70 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
957	                aria-label={t("externalSessions.moreOptionsAria")}
958	              >
959	                <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
960	              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
961	              <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-52">
962	                <DropdownMenuItem className="cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap" onClick={onOpenExternalSessions}>
963	                  <SquareTerminal className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> {t("externalSessions.title")}
964	                </DropdownMenuItem>
965	              </DropdownMenuContent>
966	            </DropdownMenu>
967	          )}
968	        </div>
969	        <div className="relative">
970	          <Search className="absolute left-2 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-[#333333] dark:text-zinc-300" />
971	          <Input
972	            type="text"
973	            placeholder={t("conversation.sidebar.searchPlaceholder")}
974	            value={searchQuery}
975	            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
976	            className="h-9 w-full border-transparent bg-[#e4e3e1] pl-8 pr-9 text-sm text-[#333333] transition-all placeholder:text-[#333333]/75 hover:bg-[#deddda] focus-visible:border-[#c8c7c5] focus-visible:bg-[#e4e3e1] focus-visible:ring-1 dark:bg-white/[0.08] dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.095] dark:focus-visible:bg-white/[0.08]"
977	          />
978	          {searchQuery ? (
979	            <Button
980	              type="button"
981	              variant="ghost"
982	              size="icon-sm"
983	              className="absolute right-1 top-1 text-[#333333]/70 hover:bg-[#d7d6d4] hover:text-[#1f1f1f] dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.12] dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
984	              aria-label={t("common.clear")}
985	              title={t("common.clear")}
986	              onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
987	              onClick={() => setSearchQuery("")}
988	            >
989	              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
990	            </Button>
991	          ) : null}
992	        </div>
993	      </div>
994	
995	      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
996	        <ScrollArea
997	          ref={scrollAreaRef}
998	          className="h-full px-3"
999	          viewportClassName="touch-pan-y touch-pinch-zoom lg:touch-auto"
1000	        >
1001	          <div className="space-y-3 pb-4 pt-0.5">
1002	          <div className="ml-2 mr-1 flex items-center justify-between">
1003	            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
1004	              <button
1005	                type="button"
1006	                onClick={() => setConversationSidebarTab("projects")}
1007	                className={cn(
1008	                  "rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",
1009	                  conversationSidebarTab === "projects"
1010	                    ? "bg-[#deddda] text-[#1f1f1f] dark:bg-white/[0.12] dark:text-zinc-100"
1011	                    : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground",
1012	                )}
1013	              >
1014	                {t("conversation.sidebar.tab.projects")}
1015	              </button>
1016	              <button
1017	                type="button"
1018	                onClick={() => setConversationSidebarTab("recent")}
1019	                className={cn(
1020	                  "rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",
1021	                  conversationSidebarTab === "recent"
1022	                    ? "bg-[#deddda] text-[#1f1f1f] dark:bg-white/[0.12] dark:text-zinc-100"
1023	                    : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground",
1024	                )}
1025	              >
1026	                {t("conversation.sidebar.tab.active")}
1027	              </button>
1028	            </div>
1029	            <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
1030	              <Tooltip>
1031	                <TooltipTrigger render={<span className="inline-flex" />}>
1032	                  <Button
1033	                    variant="ghost"
1034	                    size="icon"
1035	                    className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
1036	                    disabled={!hasExpandedVisibleProjectGroup}
1037	                    aria-label={t("conversation.sidebar.collapseAllProjects")}
1038	                    title={t("conversation.sidebar.collapseAllProjects")}
1039	                    onClick={() => onCollapseAllProjects(visibleProjectGroups.map((group) => group.path))}
1040	                  >
1041	                    <ListChevronsDownUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
1042	                  </Button>
1043	                </TooltipTrigger>
1044	                <TooltipContent side="bottom" align="end">
1045	                  {t("conversation.sidebar.collapseAllProjects")}
1046	                </TooltipContent>
1047	              </Tooltip>
1048	              <Tooltip>
1049	                <TooltipTrigger render={<span className="inline-flex" />}>
1050	                  <Button
1051	                    variant="ghost"
1052	                    size="icon"

135	function eventCreatedAtMs(event: Pick<ExecutionEventRecord, "createdAt">) {
136	  const time = new Date(event.createdAt).getTime();
137	  return Number.isFinite(time) ? time : null;
138	}
139	
140	function hasRecoverErrorClearingEvent(events: ExecutionEventRecord[], submittedAt: number | undefined) {
141	  const submittedAtMs = typeof submittedAt === "number" && Number.isFinite(submittedAt) && submittedAt > 0
142	    ? submittedAt
143	    : null;
144	  return events.some((event) => {
145	    if (!RECOVER_ERROR_CLEARING_EVENT_TYPES.has(event.eventType)) {
146	      return false;
147	    }
148	    if (submittedAtMs === null) {
149	      return true;
150	    }
151	    const eventTime = eventCreatedAtMs(event);
152	    return eventTime !== null && eventTime >= submittedAtMs;
153	  });
154	}
155	
156	/**
157	 * React Query holds the last mutation error until the mutation is reset, so an
158	 * error raised while viewing run A is still `mutation.error` after the user has
159	 * moved to run B. Conversation-scoped failures belong to their own
160	 * conversation only: mismatch → suppress. Mutations with no run in their
161	 * variables (settings, project-wide commits) stay app-global.
162	 */
163	function scopedMutationError(
164	  error: unknown,
165	  variablesRunId: string | null | undefined,
166	  selectedRunId: string | null,
167	) {
168	  if (!error) {
169	    return null;
170	  }
171	  return !variablesRunId || variablesRunId === selectedRunId ? error : null;
172	}
173	
174	function shouldShowRecoverRunError(args: {
175	  error: unknown;
176	  variablesRunId: string | null | undefined;
177	  selectedRunId: string | null;
178	  selectedRun: RunRecord | null;
179	  selectedRunExecutionEvents: ExecutionEventRecord[];
180	  submittedAt: number | undefined;
181	}) {
182	  if (!args.error || !args.variablesRunId || args.variablesRunId !== args.selectedRunId) {
183	    return false;
184	  }
185	  if (args.selectedRun && args.selectedRun.status !== "failed" && args.selectedRun.status !== "needs_recovery") {
186	    return false;
187	  }
188	  if (hasRecoverErrorClearingEvent(args.selectedRunExecutionEvents, args.submittedAt)) {
189	    return false;
190	  }
191	  return true;
192	}

932	  // Auto-resume failed runs with backoff. Up to MAX_AUTO_RESUME_ATTEMPTS per

940	  // Clear any lingering recover-run mutation error when the user

952	  const selectedAutoResumeFailureKey = selectedRun?.status === "failed"

956	    isPending: recoverRun.isPending,
957	    mutationRunId: recoverRun.variables?.runId,
958	    selectedRunId,
959	  });

961	    activeRunId: selectedRunId,

964	    failureKey: selectedAutoResumeFailureKey,

72	    case "opencode":
73	      return <OpenCodeGlyph className={className} />;
74	    default:
75	      return <SquareTerminal className={className} aria-hidden="true" />;
76	  }
77	}

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile my findings.

## Summary: Code Involved in Scrolling Active Session to Center When Mobile Sidebar Opens

### The Flow

When the mobile sidebar opens and shows the conversation list, the code automatically scrolls to center the currently-active (selected) session. Here's exactly how:

---

### Key Files and Code Locations

#### 1. **Trigger: Mobile Sidebar Open**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/HomeHeader.tsx`
   - **Lines:** 343-404
   - **Code:**
     ```tsx
     <Sheet open={mobileNavOpen} onOpenChange={setMobileNavOpen}>
       <Button ... onClick={() => setMobileNavOpen(true)}>
         <Menu className="h-4 w-4" />
       </Button>
       {mobileNavOpen ? (
         <SheetContent side="left" className="...">
           <ConversationSidebar
             runnerControlsMode="mobile"
             // ... props ...
           />
         </SheetContent>
       ) : null}
     </Sheet>
     ```
   - The `mobileNavOpen` state controls the Sheet visibility. When `true`, the sidebar is rendered with `runnerControlsMode="mobile"`.

---

#### 2. **Scroll Behavior: ConversationSidebar Component**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx`
   - **Key ref and state (lines 818-841):**
     ```tsx
     const scrollAreaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
     const scrollRestoreAttemptedRef = useRef(false);
     // ...
     const sidebarContentKey = useMemo(
       () => visibleProjectGroups
         .map((group) => `${group.path}:${group.runs.map((run) => run.id).join(",")}`)
         .join("|"),
       [visibleProjectGroups],
     );
     const getScrollViewport = useCallback(
       () => scrollAreaRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="scroll-area-viewport"]') ?? null,
       [],
     );
     ```

---

#### 3. **Scroll Save on Scroll Event (lines 847-863)**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx`
   - **Code:**
     ```tsx
     useEffect(() => {
       if (!mounted) {
         return;
       }
       const viewport = getScrollViewport();
       if (!viewport) {
         return;
       }
       const saveScrollPosition = () => {
         conversationSidebarScrollManager.setScrollTop(runnerControlsMode, viewport.scrollTop);
       };
       viewport.addEventListener("scroll", saveScrollPosition, { passive: true });
       return () => {
         saveScrollPosition();
         viewport.removeEventListener("scroll", saveScrollPosition);
       };
     }, [getScrollViewport, mounted, runnerControlsMode]);
     ```
   - Saves the current scroll position per surface ("mobile" or "desktop") whenever the user scrolls.

---

#### 4. **Core Scroll-to-Center Logic (lines 865-891)**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx`
   - **Code:**
     ```tsx
     useLayoutEffect(() => {
       if (!mounted || scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current) {
         return;
       }
       const viewport = getScrollViewport();
       if (!viewport) {
         return;
       }
       const savedScrollTop = conversationSidebarScrollManager.getScrollTop(runnerControlsMode);
       if (savedScrollTop !== undefined) {
         const maxScrollTop = Math.max(0, viewport.scrollHeight - viewport.clientHeight);
         viewport.scrollTop = Math.min(savedScrollTop, maxScrollTop);
         scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current = true;
         return;
       }
       if (runnerControlsMode !== "mobile" || !selectedRunId) {
         scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current = true;
         return;
       }
       const selectedRow = Array.from(viewport.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-conversation-run-id]"))
         .find((row) => row.dataset.conversationRunId === selectedRunId);
       if (!selectedRow) {
         return;
       }
       selectedRow.scrollIntoView({ block: "center" });
       scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current = true;
     }, [getScrollViewport, mounted, runnerControlsMode, selectedRunId, sidebarContentKey]);
     ```

   **How it works:**
   - Only runs once (guarded by `scrollRestoreAttemptedRef.current`)
   - First, checks if there's a saved scroll position for the current surface (mobile/desktop). If yes, restores it and exits.
   - **If mobile mode AND a session is selected:** finds the DOM element with `[data-conversation-run-id={selectedRunId}]` and calls `scrollIntoView({ block: "center" })` to center it.
   - **Dependency on `sidebarContentKey`:** When the conversation list changes (projects/runs reorganize), the effect re-runs but only after the view is remounted (first return guards prevent re-execution).

---

#### 5. **The ScrollArea Container (lines 995-1000)**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx`
   - **Code:**
     ```tsx
     <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
       <ScrollArea
         ref={scrollAreaRef}
         className="h-full px-3"
         viewportClassName="touch-pan-y touch-pinch-zoom lg:touch-auto"
       >
         {/* conversation list */}
       </ScrollArea>
     </div>
     ```
   - The `scrollAreaRef` is attached here, which the scroll logic queries for the viewport.

---

#### 6. **ScrollArea Component Implementation**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/ui/scroll-area.tsx`
   - **Code (lines 7-33):**
     ```tsx
     function ScrollArea({
       className,
       viewportClassName,
       children,
       ref,
       ...props
     }: React.ComponentPropsWithRef<"div"> & { viewportClassName?: string }) {
       return (
         <div
           data-slot="scroll-area"
           ref={ref}
           className={cn("relative overflow-hidden", className)}
           {...props}
         >
           <div
             data-slot="scroll-area-viewport"
             className={cn(
               "size-full overflow-auto rounded-[inherit] transition-[color,box-shadow] outline-none focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-1 [scrollbar-width:thin]",
               viewportClassName,
             )}
             tabIndex={props.tabIndex ?? 0}
           >
             {children}
           </div>
         </div>
       )
     }
     ```
   - The `[data-slot="scroll-area-viewport"]` is the actual scrollable element queried by `getScrollViewport()`.

---

#### 7. **Conversation Row Elements**
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx` (lines 440-592)
   - **Relevant code (lines 441-456):**
     ```tsx
     <div
       key={run.id}
       data-conversation-run-id={run.id}
       onClick={() => selectRun(run.id)}
       // ...
       className={cn(
         "group flex min-w-0 cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-xl py-1.5 pl-2.5 pr-2 text-sm transition-colors",
         selectedRunId === run.id
           ? "bg-[#e2e1df] text-[#1f1f1f] dark:bg-white/[0.08] dark:text-zinc-100"
           : "text-[#424242] hover:bg-[#e8e7e5] hover:text-[#1f1f1f] dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.045] dark:hover:text-zinc-100",
       )}
     >
     ```
   - Each conversation row has `data-conversation-run-id={run.id}`, allowing the scroll logic to find and target it.
   - Contains a `CliBrandIcon` (line 489) which may load an external PNG for "opencode" worker type.

---

### State Management: ConversationSidebarScrollManager
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx` (lines 43-63)
   - **Code:**
     ```tsx
     class ConversationSidebarScrollManager extends StateManager<{
       scrollTopBySurface: Partial<Record<ConversationSidebarSurface, number>>;
     }> {
       constructor() {
         super({ scrollTopBySurface: {} });
       }

       getScrollTop(surface: ConversationSidebarSurface) {
         return this.getSnapshot().scrollTopBySurface[surface];
       }

       setScrollTop(surface: ConversationSidebarSurface, scrollTop: number) {
         this.setKey("scrollTopBySurface", (current) => (
           current[surface] === scrollTop
             ? current
             : { ...current, [surface]: scrollTop }
         ));
       }
     }

     const conversationSidebarScrollManager = new ConversationSidebarScrollManager();
     ```
   - Tracks scroll position separately for "mobile" and "desktop" surfaces.

---

### Animation & Transition Timing
   - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/ui/sheet.tsx` (line 56)
   - The `SheetContent` has:
     ```tsx
     "transition duration-200 ease-in-out data-ending-style:opacity-0 data-starting-style:opacity-0 data-[side=left]:data-ending-style:translate-x-[-2.5rem] data-[side=left]:data-starting-style:translate-x-[-2.5rem]"
     ```
   - The sidebar slides in over ~200ms. The scroll-to-center runs immediately after the sidebar mounts (via `useLayoutEffect` which runs after DOM updates but before paint).

---

### Potential Issues: Layout Shifts and Lazy Image Loading
   - **CliBrandIcon for OpenCode (lines 51-62 in cli-brand-icons.tsx):**
     ```tsx
     function OpenCodeGlyph({ className }: { className?: string }) {
       return (
         <span className={cn("relative inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden", className)}>
           <img
             src={OPENCODE_ICON_SRC}
             alt=""
             aria-hidden="true"
             className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
           />
         </span>
       );
     }
     ```
   - **Issue:** The PNG image at `/brand-icons/opencode.png` has no `onLoad` handler. If it hasn't loaded by the time the scroll-to-center runs, row heights could shift after the scroll completes, causing the active session to move out of view.
   - **No virtualization:** The conversation list is fully rendered (not using react-window or similar), so layout shifts affect all rows.

---

### Dependencies & Trigger Conditions
- **Dependencies of the scroll effect:** `[getScrollViewport, mounted, runnerControlsMode, selectedRunId, sidebarContentKey]`
- **Trigger:** The effect fires when:
  1. Component mounts
  2. `runnerControlsMode` changes (e.g., desktop → mobile when sidebar opens)
  3. `selectedRunId` changes
  4. `sidebarContentKey` changes (conversation list reorganized)

**Note:** `mobileNavOpen` is NOT a dependency. The effect runs based on `runnerControlsMode` changing to "mobile" and the sidebar being conditionally rendered in HomeHeader, so by the time the scroll logic runs, the DOM is already present.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-26T10:26:10.834Z

I want to check the actual code before concluding. The user's complaint about the row ending up higher than expected likely points to scrollIntoView being called before layout settles, or a scroll container conflict causing the offset to be off.

That clicks — if clientHeight is small when scrollTop gets computed, the row ends up positioned for that smaller viewport, and once the layout grows the row appears above center, matching the reported bug. A more robust fix would be to compute scrollTop manually rather than relying on scrollIntoView, and re-run the calculation after layout truly settles using rAF or a ResizeObserver, while also accounting for scrollIntoView scrolling unintended ancestor elements. I should go look at the actual file now to confirm this theory.

Actually there's a bigger issue: the scroll-restore logic only fires once per attempt, and it prioritizes any saved scrollTop over centering the newly selected run. That means once a user has scrolled the mobile sidebar, reopening it after switching sessions would restore the old scroll position instead of centering the new selection — which could explain why centering only works "some" of the time, since the restored position depends on how close the old and new selections are in the list.

I want to actually read through the sidebar code and the modified mobile-layout e2e spec to confirm this rather than keep speculating.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-26T10:26:27.911Z

I'm spotting the core bug: saved scroll position takes priority over centering, so once the mobile sidebar has a stored scroll value, reopening it restores that stale position instead of centering on the newly selected session—explaining the inconsistent "works sometimes" behavior.

I also notice `scrollIntoView({block:"center"})` can scroll ancestor elements unintentionally, and the layout effect runs before the sheet's transition finishes, risking stale height measurements. The real fix should distinguish desktop's persistent scroll restoration from mobile's need to always center on open.

I'm thinking the cleanest approach is to store the selected run ID alongside the saved scroll position, only restoring when they match, otherwise centering; then compute scrollTop directly on the viewport instead of using scrollIntoView, and re-run the correction after layout settles via requestAnimationFrame. Let me check whether the desktop sidebar unmounts on collapse and look for existing tests covering this scroll behavior, like mobile-layout.spec.ts.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-26T10:27:06.262Z

I'm identifying the primary bug: once mobile scrollTop gets saved after the first open/close, that stale value short-circuits centering on later opens, so if the selection changed since then, the view lands near the old row instead of the current one. I'm now considering whether the user switches sessions through the same interaction path that triggers this save.

For the secondary bug, `scrollIntoView({block:"center"})` inside a useLayoutEffect is problematic because it scrolls every ancestor container and can fire before layout has fully settled, which could explain the small vertical offset the user notices.

I'm thinking the cleanest fix is to save the selected run's id alongside the scroll position on mobile, so on reopening we only restore that saved scroll if the selection hasn't changed since — otherwise we re-center on the newly selected run. That preserves both the "remembers scroll across drawer remounts" behavior and correct centering when the selection changes.

I'm also replacing the scrollIntoView call with a manual scrollTop calculation using getBoundingClientRect on the row versus the viewport, so centering is computed relative to the viewport itself rather than relying on ancestor offsets, avoiding the correction issue when content reflows late.

I want a more robust approach: keep centering active with rAF re-checks until the drawer's transition finishes or the user manually interacts (scroll/pointerdown), correcting scrollTop if it drifts from the target by more than a pixel. I'll track this with a ref that stays active for roughly the transition duration, using rAF ticks and a ResizeObserver to catch layout shifts, while making sure the scroll-position-save listener doesn't interfere during that window.

I also need to check how the component behaves on mobile with the ScrollArea before implementing, account for the initial render where the viewport isn't mounted yet, and confirm the one-shot scroll-restoration flag still allows for these settle-window corrections. Then I'll dig into the git history to understand why the saved-scroll logic was added in the first place.
